- **clarify-first**: This skill should be used when a request is ambiguous, underspecified, conflicting, or high impact. It should not be used for purely informational requests or explicitly scoped low-risk changes. The skill enforces risk triage, blocking clarification, explicit confirmation for medium/high-risk work, and prevents silent assumptions before execution. - **clarify-first**: Resolve unclear instructions before acting.
